"""Targeted trainer (innovation-aware)."""
import argparse
import json
import os
import sys
import time

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl"))
for _v in ("OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS", "OMP_NUM_THREADS",
           "NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS", "VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS"):
    os.environ.setdefault(_v, "1")

import numpy as np

from uav_wireless.experiments import base_config, eval_agent
from uav_wireless.train import train_mappo

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
MLIMPL = os.path.join(HERE, "..", "ml_impl")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--seeds", nargs="+", type=int, default=[0])
    ap.add_argument("--episodes", type=int, default=200)
    ap.add_argument("--n-steps", type=int, default=2000)
    ap.add_argument("--ego", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--n-ego", type=int, default=3)
    ap.add_argument("--phy-ego", action="store_true",
                    help="PHY-aware ego bearings and self-served-sensor exclusion")
    ap.add_argument("--assist-return", action="store_true",
                    help="force a pad-approaching move in return mode")
    ap.add_argument("--scalable-id", action="store_true",
                    help="N-invariant 3-dim agent identity")
    ap.add_argument("--per-agent-adv", action="store_true",
                    help="normalise each agent's advantages over its own history")
    ap.add_argument("--domain-rand", action="store_true",
                    help="mix clustered, small-battery, low-comm and failure scenes")
    ap.add_argument("--no-agent-id", action="store_true",
                    help="drop the N-dimensional one-hot identity")
    ap.add_argument("--anchor", action="store_true",
                    help="add the critical-coverage anchor feature")
    ap.add_argument("--shape", type=float, default=0.0)
    ap.add_argument("--energy-kappa", type=float, default=0.0)
    ap.add_argument("--intrinsic-beta", type=float, default=0.0)
    ap.add_argument("--no-w", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--credit-mode", default="team")
    ap.add_argument("--diff-beta", type=float, default=1.0)
    ap.add_argument("--randomize-layout", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--adversarial", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--curriculum", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--rf-at", type=int, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--lr-decay", type=float, default=0.5)
    ap.add_argument("--eval-seeds", type=int, default=20)
    ap.add_argument("--eval-offset", type=int, default=1000)
    ap.add_argument("--tag", default="exp")
    ap.add_argument("--attn", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--action-repeat", type=int, default=1)
    ap.add_argument("--device", default=None,
                    help="cpu or cuda; default lets torch choose")
    ap.add_argument("--regime", default="nominal",
                    help="stress regime name (nominal, small_battery, "
                         "low_comm, failure, etc.)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    outdir = os.path.join(MLIMPL, "runs", args.tag)
    os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)

    for s in args.seeds:
        out_path = os.path.join(outdir, "seed_%d.json" % s)
        if os.path.exists(out_path):
            print("seed %d: already done, skipping" % s, flush=True)
            continue
        if args.regime and args.regime != "nominal":
            import sys as _sys
            _sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__)) )
            from stress_regimes import get_config as _gc
            cfg = _gc(args.regime)
        else:
            cfg = base_config()
        cfg.episode_len = args.n_steps
        cfg.credit_mode = args.credit_mode
        cfg.diff_beta = args.diff_beta
        cfg.ego_sensors = args.ego
        cfg.n_ego = args.n_ego
        cfg.phy_ego_sensors = args.phy_ego
        cfg.assist_return_mask = args.assist_return
        cfg.use_scalable_id = args.scalable_id
        if args.no_agent_id:
            cfg.use_agent_id = False
        cfg.use_critical_anchor = args.anchor
        cfg.dist_shape_kappa = args.shape
        cfg.energy_shape_kappa = args.energy_kappa
        cfg.intrinsic_beta = args.intrinsic_beta
        cfg.use_w_penalty = not args.no_w
        cfg.randomize_layout = args.randomize_layout
        cfg.adversarial_layout = args.adversarial

        t0 = time.time()
        agent, hist = train_mappo(cfg, episodes=args.episodes,
                                  n_steps=args.n_steps, seed=s, verbose=True,
                                  log_every=20, epochs=10, n_minibatch=4,
                                  per_agent_adv_norm=args.per_agent_adv,
                                  domain_rand=args.domain_rand,
                                  curriculum=args.curriculum,
                                  rf_at=args.rf_at, lr_decay=args.lr_decay,
                                  attn=args.attn,
                                  action_repeat=args.action_repeat,
                                  device=args.device,
                                  ckpt_dir=outdir, ckpt_every=25)
        dt = time.time() - t0
        agent.save(os.path.join(outdir, "seed_%d.pt" % s), cfg=cfg)

        scenes = list(range(args.eval_offset, args.eval_offset + args.eval_seeds))
        ev = []
        for es in scenes:
            r = eval_agent(agent, cfg, steps=args.n_steps, seed=es,
                           deterministic=False)
            ev.append(float(r["CK"]))
        evd = [float(eval_agent(agent, cfg, steps=args.n_steps, seed=es,
                                deterministic=True)["CK"])
               for es in scenes]
        cks = ev
        rec = {
            "seed": s, "episodes": args.episodes, "n_steps": args.n_steps,
            "seconds": dt, "ego": args.ego, "n_ego": args.n_ego,
            "phy_ego": args.phy_ego, "assist_return": args.assist_return,
            "scalable_id": args.scalable_id, "use_agent_id": cfg.use_agent_id,
            "per_agent_adv": args.per_agent_adv, "domain_rand": args.domain_rand,
            "anchor": args.anchor,
            "shape": args.shape, "energy_kappa": args.energy_kappa,
            "use_w": not args.no_w, "credit_mode": args.credit_mode,
            "diff_beta": args.diff_beta, "curriculum": args.curriculum,
            "rf_at": args.rf_at, "randomize_layout": args.randomize_layout,
            "train_ck": float(hist[-1].get("CK", 0.0)) if hist else None,
            "eval_seeds": scenes, "eval_offset": args.eval_offset,
            "eval_ck": float(np.mean(cks)),
            "eval_ck_std": float(np.std(cks, ddof=1)) if len(cks) > 1 else 0.0,
            "eval_per_seed": cks, "eval_det_ck": float(np.mean(evd)),
            "final_ck": float(np.mean(cks)),
            "hist": hist,
        }
        with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(rec, f)
        print("seed %d: DONE %.0fs | eval CK=%.4f (sd %.4f) det=%.4f train_ck=%.4f"
              % (s, dt, rec["eval_ck"], rec["eval_ck_std"],
                 rec["eval_det_ck"], rec["train_ck"]), flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

==============================================================================
=== scripts/eval_ckpt.py  (130 lines)
==============================================================================
"""Evaluate saved checkpoints on a controlled set of scenes.

Evaluation is decoupled from training on purpose.  A policy is trained once and
then measured as many times as the protocol needs, on scene sets chosen after
the fact, without retraining: the headline number of the paper is the frozen
policy's mean over a set of scenes, and a number that required retraining to
recompute is a number that gets frozen by accident.

Scene selection
---------------
A scene is a function of the environment seed.  Training consumes seeds
0, 1, 2, ... so the evaluation scenes are drawn from an offset range
(default 1000..) that training never touches: no evaluation scene is a
training scene, which is what "held out" has to mean if it is to mean
anything.

Run:
    python scripts/eval_ckpt.py --tag exp_ego_eval --eval-offset 1000 \
        --eval-seeds 20
"""
import argparse
import glob
import json
import os
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl"))
for _v in ("OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS", "OMP_NUM_THREADS",
           "NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS", "VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS"):
    os.environ.setdefault(_v, "1")

import numpy as np
import torch

from uav_wireless.env import UAVIoTEnv
from uav_wireless.experiments import base_config, eval_agent
from uav_wireless.mappo import MAPPO


def build_agent(cfg, ckpt):
    env = UAVIoTEnv(cfg)
    agent = MAPPO(n_agents=cfg.N, obs_dim=env.obs_dim,
                  state_dim=env.state_dim, device="cpu")
    agent.load(ckpt)
    return agent


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--tag", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--eval-offset", type=int, default=1000)
    ap.add_argument("--eval-seeds", type=int, default=20)
    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="")
    a = ap.parse_args()

    rundir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl", "runs",
                          a.tag)
    ckpts = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(rundir, "seed_*.pt")))
    if not ckpts:
        print("no checkpoints in", rundir)
        return 1

    scenes = list(range(a.eval_offset, a.eval_offset + a.eval_seeds))
    results = {}
    for ck in ckpts:
        # the configuration is recovered from the training record, so that a
        # checkpoint is always evaluated with the environment it was trained
        # in and never with the default one
        meta_path = ck.replace(".pt", ".json")
        with open(meta_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            meta = json.load(f)
        cfg = base_config()
        cfg.ego_sensors = meta.get("ego", False)
        cfg.n_ego = meta.get("n_ego", 3)
        cfg.phy_ego_sensors = meta.get("phy_ego", False)
        cfg.assist_return_mask = meta.get("assist_return", False)
        cfg.use_scalable_id = meta.get("scalable_id", False)
        if "use_agent_id" in meta:
            cfg.use_agent_id = bool(meta["use_agent_id"])
        cfg.dist_shape_kappa = meta.get("shape", 0.0)
        cfg.use_w_penalty = meta.get("use_w", True)
        cfg.credit_mode = meta.get("credit_mode", "team")
        cfg.randomize_layout = meta.get("randomize_layout", False)
        cfg.use_critical_anchor = meta.get("anchor", False)
        steps = a.steps or meta.get("n_steps", 2000)
        cfg.episode_len = steps

        agent = build_agent(cfg, ck)
        per, det = [], []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for s in scenes:
                r = eval_agent(agent, cfg, steps=steps, seed=s,
                               deterministic=False)
                per.append(float(r["CK"]))
                det.append(float(eval_agent(agent, cfg, steps=steps, seed=s,
                                            deterministic=True)["CK"]))
        name = os.path.basename(ck)
        results[name] = {
            "scenes": scenes, "per_scene_CK": per,
            "mean": float(np.mean(per)),
            "std": float(np.std(per, ddof=1)) if len(per) > 1 else 0.0,
            "det_mean": float(np.mean(det)),
        }
        print("%-14s CK=%.4f (sd over scenes %.4f)  argmax %.4f"
              % (name, results[name]["mean"], results[name]["std"],
                 results[name]["det_mean"]), flush=True)

    means = [r["mean"] for r in results.values()]
    summary = {
        "tag": a.tag, "eval_offset": a.eval_offset,
        "eval_seeds": a.eval_seeds, "steps": a.steps or None,
        "per_checkpoint": results,
        "mean_over_seeds": float(np.mean(means)),
        "sd_over_seeds": float(np.std(means, ddof=1)) if len(means) > 1 else 0.0,
        "n_seeds": len(means),
    }
    out = a.out or os.path.join(rundir, "eval_heldout.json")
    with open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(summary, f, indent=1)
    print("\n%s: mean over %d seeds = %.4f (sd %.4f)"
          % (a.tag, len(means), summary["mean_over_seeds"],
             summary["sd_over_seeds"]))
    print("wrote", out)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

==============================================================================
=== scripts/eval_stress.py  (190 lines)
==============================================================================
"""Zero-shot stress evaluation of the corrected learner.

The learner is trained on the nominal configuration and then rolled out,
without retraining, on each stress regime of scripts/stress_regimes.py.  The
rules are measured on the same scenes.  This is the experiment that decides
whether the thesis ``certifying where learning does not help'' is supported
on stress or only on the base configuration.

Writes ml_impl/runs/stress_eval/stress_eval.json
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
import sys
try:
    import torch
    torch.set_num_threads(1)
except Exception:
    pass

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl"))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__)))
for _v in ("OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS", "OMP_NUM_THREADS",
           "NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS", "VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS"):
    os.environ.setdefault(_v, "1")

import numpy as np
import torch

from uav_wireless.env import UAVIoTEnv
from uav_wireless.experiments import base_config, eval_agent
from uav_wireless.mappo import MAPPO
from uav_wireless.train import (baseline_fixed, baseline_greedy,
                                baseline_reactive, baseline_reactive_local)
from stress_regimes import get_config, REGIMES

# the learner checkpoints were trained in this configuration (ego
# bearings + distance shaping); this must be applied on top of every
# regime override when the learner is evaluated.
LEARNER_ANCHOR = dict(ego_sensors=True, n_ego=3,
                      dist_shape_kappa=0.5, use_w_penalty=True,
                      use_critical_anchor=False)

def learner_anchor_from_meta(ckpt_list):
    """Read the learner config flags from the first checkpoint's training
    metadata, so stress evaluation uses the same observation the policy
    was trained with (incl. the critical-coverage anchor feature)."""
    import json as _j
    anchor = dict(LEARNER_ANCHOR)
    if ckpt_list:
        try:
            meta = _j.load(open(ckpt_list[0].replace(".pt", ".json"), encoding="utf-8"))
            anchor["ego_sensors"] = meta.get("ego", True)
            anchor["n_ego"] = meta.get("n_ego", 3)
            anchor["dist_shape_kappa"] = meta.get("shape", 0.5)
            anchor["use_w_penalty"] = meta.get("use_w", True)
            anchor["use_critical_anchor"] = meta.get("anchor", False)
            anchor["phy_ego_sensors"] = meta.get("phy_ego", False)
            anchor["assist_return_mask"] = meta.get("assist_return", False)
            anchor["use_scalable_id"] = meta.get("scalable_id", False)
            if "use_agent_id" in meta:
                anchor["use_agent_id"] = bool(meta["use_agent_id"])
        except Exception:
            pass
    return anchor

# Frozen evaluation only uses the actor, so regimes are compatible when
# their local-observation dimensions match the trained policy. The critic
# is used for training, not action selection.
ACTOR_COMPATIBLE_REGIMES = {"nominal", "single_pad", "small_battery",
                            "low_comm", "failure", "multi_failure",
                            "clustered"}

RULES_ALL = [("Fixed", baseline_fixed), ("Greedy", baseline_greedy),
            ("Reactive", baseline_reactive), ("Reactive-local", baseline_reactive_local)]
RULES = None  # set from --rules


def eval_learner_on_regime(ckpt_list, cfg, scenes, steps):
    per_seed = []
    for ck in ckpt_list:
        import json as _j
        meta = _j.load(open(ck.replace(".pt", ".json"), encoding="utf-8"))
        # build env to get dims
        env = UAVIoTEnv(cfg)
        ag = MAPPO(n_agents=cfg.N, obs_dim=env.obs_dim,
                   state_dim=env.state_dim, device="cpu")
        ag.load_actor(ck)
        cks = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for s in scenes:
                r = eval_agent(ag, cfg, steps=steps, seed=s, deterministic=False)
                cks.append(float(r["CK"]))
        per_seed.append(float(np.mean(cks)))
    return per_seed


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--tag", default="exp_ego_eval")
    ap.add_argument("--eval-offset", type=int, default=1000)
    ap.add_argument("--eval-seeds", type=int, default=5)
    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=2000)
    ap.add_argument("--only", nargs="+", default=None,
                    help="only these regimes")
    ap.add_argument("--rules", nargs="+",
                    default=["Fixed", "Reactive-local"])
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="")
    a = ap.parse_args()

    rundir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl", "runs", a.tag)
    ckpts = sorted(__import__("glob").glob(os.path.join(rundir, "seed_*.pt")))
    if not ckpts:
        print("no checkpoints in", rundir)
        return 1
    scenes = list(range(a.eval_offset, a.eval_offset + a.eval_seeds))
    results = {}
    global RULES
    RULES = [x for x in RULES_ALL if x[0] in a.rules]
    regimes = a.only or list(REGIMES.keys())
    for regime in regimes:
        print("\n=== regime:", regime, "===", flush=True)
        rule_cfg = get_config(regime)
        rule_cfg.episode_len = a.steps
        learner_cfg = get_config(regime)
        _anchor_flags = learner_anchor_from_meta(ckpts)
        for _k, _v in _anchor_flags.items():
            setattr(learner_cfg, _k, _v)
        learner_cfg.episode_len = a.steps
        # rules
        rule_summary = {}
        for name, fn in RULES:
            cks = [float(fn(rule_cfg, steps=a.steps, seed=s)["CK"]) for s in scenes]
            rule_summary[name] = {"CK": float(np.mean(cks)),
                                  "sd": float(np.std(cks, ddof=1)),
                                  "per_scene": cks}
        best_rule = max(rule_summary.values(), key=lambda v: v["CK"])
        best_name = max(rule_summary, key=lambda k: rule_summary[k]["CK"])
        # learner (its own anchor config on top of the regime); only where
        # the architecture dims are unchanged
        if regime in ACTOR_COMPATIBLE_REGIMES:
            learner_per_seed = eval_learner_on_regime(ckpts, learner_cfg, scenes, a.steps)
        else:
            learner_per_seed = []
        learner_mean = (float(np.mean(learner_per_seed))
                        if learner_per_seed else float("nan"))
        learner_sd = (float(np.std(learner_per_seed, ddof=1))
                      if len(learner_per_seed) > 1 else 0.0)
        headroom = 1.0 - rule_summary[best_name]["CK"]
        results[regime] = {
            "rules": rule_summary,
            "best_rule": best_name,
            "best_rule_ck": rule_summary[best_name]["CK"],
            "learner_mean": learner_mean,
            "learner_sd": learner_sd,
            "learner_per_seed": learner_per_seed,
            "headroom": headroom,
            "learner_minus_rule": (learner_mean - rule_summary[best_name]["CK"]
                                   if learner_per_seed else None),
        }
        print("  best rule %-16s CK=%.4f  headroom=%.4f" % (best_name, rule_summary[best_name]["CK"], headroom))
        print("  learner (zero-shot) CK=%.4f (sd %.4f)" % (learner_mean, learner_sd))
        print("  learner - rule = %+.4f" % (learner_mean - rule_summary[best_name]["CK"]))

    outdir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl", "runs", "stress_eval")
    os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)
    out = a.out or os.path.join(outdir, "stress_eval.json")
    # merge with an existing artifact (the --only runs update single regimes)
    merged = {}
    if os.path.exists(out):
        try:
            merged = json.load(open(out, encoding="utf-8")).get("regimes", {})
        except Exception:
            merged = {}
    merged.update(results)
    with open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"regimes": merged, "scenes": scenes, "steps": a.steps,
                   "tag": a.tag, "n_seeds": len(ckpts)}, f, indent=1)
    print("\nwrote", out)
    # summary table
    print("\n%-16s %-18s %8s %8s %8s" % ("regime", "best rule", "rule CK", "learner", "headroom"))
    for r, v in results.items():
        print("%-16s %-18s %8.4f %8.4f %8.4f" % (r, v["best_rule"], v["best_rule_ck"],
              v["learner_mean"], v["headroom"]))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

==============================================================================
=== scripts/eval_rules.py  (98 lines)
==============================================================================
"""Evaluate the rule baselines over a set of evaluation scenes.

The report compares a learned policy against four deterministic rules.  For
that comparison to be a comparison, both sides have to be measured over the
SAME set of scenes: the rules are deterministic given a scene, so their
seed-to-seed spread is scene-to-scene spread, and a single-scene number for
a rule is not comparable with a multi-scene mean for a learner.

Writes ml_impl/runs/rules/eval_rules.json:

    {"eval_seeds": [0..n-1],
     "rules": {"Fixed": [ck per seed], ...},
     "summary": {"Fixed": {"CK": mean, "CK_std": sample sd}, ...}}

Run:  python scripts/eval_rules.py --eval-seeds 10
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl"))
for _v in ("OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS", "OMP_NUM_THREADS",
           "NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS", "VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS"):
    os.environ.setdefault(_v, "1")

import numpy as np

from uav_wireless.experiments import base_config
from uav_wireless.train import (baseline_fixed, baseline_greedy,
                                baseline_reactive, baseline_reactive_local)

RULES = [
    ("Fixed", baseline_fixed),
    ("Greedy", baseline_greedy),
    ("Reactive", baseline_reactive),
    ("Reactive-local", baseline_reactive_local),
]

FIELDS = ("CK", "AG", "SC", "Energy", "Distance", "UnexecutedActions",
          "CovIntegral", "ReturnEvents", "Stranded")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--eval-seeds", type=int, default=10)
    ap.add_argument("--eval-offset", type=int, default=1000,
                    help="first scene seed; the default range is disjoint "
                         "from the training seeds 0.. so that no evaluation "
                         "scene was ever trained on")
    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=2000)
    ap.add_argument("--tag", default="rules")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    outdir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl",
                          "runs", args.tag)
    os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)
    out = os.path.join(outdir, "eval_rules.json")

    cfg = base_config()
    steps = args.steps or cfg.episode_len
    seeds = list(range(args.eval_offset, args.eval_offset + args.eval_seeds))
    rec = {"eval_seeds": seeds, "eval_offset": args.eval_offset,
           "steps": steps, "rules": {}, "summary": {}}
    for name, fn in RULES:
        per = []
        for s in rec["eval_seeds"]:
            r = fn(cfg, steps=steps, seed=s)
            per.append({k: (float(r[k]) if k in r and r[k] is not None else None)
                        for k in FIELDS})
        rec["rules"][name] = per
        cks = [p["CK"] for p in per]
        rec["summary"][name] = {
            "CK": float(np.mean(cks)),
            "CK_std": float(np.std(cks, ddof=1)) if len(cks) > 1 else 0.0,
            "n": len(cks),
            "per_seed_CK": cks,
        }
        for k in ("AG", "SC"):
            vals = [p[k] for p in per if p[k] is not None]
            if vals:
                rec["summary"][name][k] = float(np.mean(vals))
        for k in ("Energy", "Distance", "UnexecutedActions"):
            vals = [p[k] for p in per if p[k] is not None]
            if vals:
                rec["summary"][name][k] = float(np.mean(vals))
        print("%-16s CK=%.4f (sd %.4f) n=%d"
              % (name, rec["summary"][name]["CK"],
                 rec["summary"][name]["CK_std"], len(cks)), flush=True)

    with open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(rec, f, indent=1)
    print("wrote", out)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

==============================================================================
=== scripts/eval_planning.py  (154 lines)
==============================================================================
"""Innovation 10: feasibility-aware planning (H-step lookahead at eval time).

For each decision, instead of sampling one action, sample K candidate action
vectors and roll each forward H steps in a cloned environment, scoring by
cumulative coverage.  Pick the best candidate.  This is model-based planning
with the feasibility mask as the constraint.

Run:  python scripts/eval_planning.py --tag exp_ego_eval --eval-offset 1000 --eval-seeds 5 --H 3 --K 8
"""
import argparse
import copy
import glob
import json
import os
import sys
import time

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl"))
for _v in ("OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS", "OMP_NUM_THREADS",
           "NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS", "VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS"):
    os.environ.setdefault(_v, "1")

import numpy as np
import torch

from uav_wireless.env import UAVIoTEnv
from uav_wireless.experiments import base_config, eval_agent
from uav_wireless.mappo import MAPPO


def rollout_score(env, agent, H, deterministic_seed):
    """Roll out H steps with the policy, return cumulative CK."""
    obs, st = env.local_obs() if False else None, None
    # we already have env state; just step
    obs = env.local_obs() if hasattr(env, 'local_obs') else None
    # env.local_obs doesn't exist; use env's obs via reset? No, env already reset.
    # We'll get obs from the env's last obs - but env doesn't store it.
    # Re-derive: env has p, v, etc. We need obs. Let's call env.local_obs if exists.
    if not hasattr(env, "local_obs"):
        return 0.0
    obs = env.local_obs()
    mask = np.stack(env.action_masks())
    for i in range(env.cfg.N):
        if mask[i].sum() == 0:
            mask[i, 0] = True
    acts = agent.act(obs, mask, deterministic=True)
    cum = 0.0
    for _ in range(H):
        obs, st, r, done, _ = env.step(acts)
        cum += float(env.summary().get("CK", 0.0))
        if done:
            break
        mask = np.stack(env.action_masks())
        for i in range(env.cfg.N):
            if mask[i].sum() == 0:
                mask[i, 0] = True
        acts = agent.act(obs, mask, deterministic=True)
    return cum


def planned_eval(agent, cfg, steps, seed, H, K):
    import copy
    c = copy.deepcopy(cfg)
    c.seed = seed
    from uav_wireless.seeding import seed_evaluation
    seed_evaluation(seed)
    env = UAVIoTEnv(c)
    obs, st = env.reset()
    cum_ck = 0.0
    n_steps = 0
    while n_steps < steps:
        mask = np.stack(env.action_masks())
        for i in range(c.N):
            if mask[i].sum() == 0:
                mask[i, 0] = True
        # sample K candidate actions
        logits = agent.masked_logits(
            torch.as_tensor(obs, dtype=torch.float32),
            torch.as_tensor(mask, dtype=torch.bool))
        logp = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)
        best_acts = None
        best_score = -1e9
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed * 999 + n_steps)
        for _ in range(K):
            cand = torch.distributions.Categorical(logits=logp).sample().cpu().numpy()
            # clone env
            env2 = copy.deepcopy(env)
            obs2 = obs.copy() if hasattr(obs, "copy") else obs
            # score by H-step rollout
            sc = 0.0
            o = obs2
            m = mask.copy()
            a = cand.copy()
            for h in range(H):
                o2, st2, r, done, _ = env2.step(a)
                sc += float(env2.summary().get("CK", 0.0))
                if done:
                    break
                m = np.stack(env2.action_masks())
                for i in range(c.N):
                    if m[i].sum() == 0:
                        m[i, 0] = True
                a = agent.act(o2, m, deterministic=True)
                o = o2
            if sc > best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_acts = cand.copy()
        if best_acts is None:
            best_acts = agent.act(obs, mask, deterministic=True)
        obs, st, r, done, _ = env.step(best_acts)
        n_steps += 1
        cum_ck += float(env.summary().get("CK", 0.0))  # per-step, not cumulative
    # return mean per-step CK (approx)
    return float(env.summary().get("CK", 0.0))


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--tag", default="exp_ego_eval")
    ap.add_argument("--eval-offset", type=int, default=1000)
    ap.add_argument("--eval-seeds", type=int, default=5)
    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=500)
    ap.add_argument("--H", type=int, default=3)
    ap.add_argument("--K", type=int, default=4)
    a = ap.parse_args()

    rundir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl", "runs", a.tag)
    ckpts = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(rundir, "seed_*.pt")))
    scenes = list(range(a.eval_offset, a.eval_offset + a.eval_seeds))
    print("planning H=%d K=%d on %d scenes" % (a.H, a.K, len(scenes)))
    for ck in ckpts:
        meta = json.load(open(ck.replace(".pt", ".json"), encoding="utf-8"))
        cfg = base_config()
        cfg.ego_sensors = meta.get("ego", False)
        cfg.n_ego = meta.get("n_ego", 3)
        cfg.dist_shape_kappa = meta.get("shape", 0.0)
        cfg.use_w_penalty = meta.get("use_w", True)
        cfg.episode_len = a.steps
        env = UAVIoTEnv(cfg)
        agent = MAPPO(n_agents=cfg.N, obs_dim=env.obs_dim,
                      state_dim=env.state_dim, device="cpu")
        agent.load(ck)
        cks = []
        for s in scenes:
            c = planned_eval(agent, cfg, a.steps, s, a.H, a.K)
            cks.append(c)
        print("%s  planned CK=%.4f (sd %.4f)" % (os.path.basename(ck),
              float(np.mean(cks)), float(np.std(cks, ddof=1))))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

==============================================================================
=== scripts/eval_classical_baseline.py  (167 lines)
==============================================================================
"""
Classical baseline: oracle-formation + Hungarian assignment + charging rotation.

A strong non-learning controller for persistent K-coverage:
  1. Precompute a connected K-covering formation via the teleporting
     oracle (search_best with connected_only=True).
  2. Each step, assign active (non-charging) UAVs to formation slots via
     the Hungarian algorithm (minimise total distance).
  3. Move toward the assigned slot; hover if arrived.
  4. Battery: if E_i <= return_threshold, the UAV returns to the nearest
     pad and charges.  Slots vacated by charging UAVs are filled by the
     next available charged UAV (relieve-before-leave is approximated by
     only allowing one UAV per slot to charge at a time).

This is the operations-research-style scheduler a reviewer will ask for:
it should score near the oracle on nominal and expose where learning adds
value (connectivity failures, multi-failure recovery).

Run:
    python scripts/eval_classical_baseline.py --tag exp_ego_eval \\
        --eval-offset 1000 --eval-seeds 5 --steps 500
"""
import argparse
import copy
import glob
import json
import os
import sys
import time

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl"))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl", "analysis"))
for _v in ("OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS", "OMP_NUM_THREADS",
           "NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS", "VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS"):
    os.environ.setdefault(_v, "1")

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import linear_sum_assignment
from uav_wireless.env import UAVIoTEnv, SimConfig
from uav_wireless.experiments import base_config
from feasibility import search_best, is_connected_at

# action constants
A_HOVER, A_PX, A_NX, A_PY, A_NY, A_CHARGE = 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5
DIRS = {A_PX: (1, 0), A_NX: (-1, 0), A_PY: (0, 1), A_NY: (0, -1)}


def move_toward(env, i, target):
    """Return the cardinal action that moves UAV i toward target."""
    dx = target[0] - env.p[i][0]
    dy = target[1] - env.p[i][1]
    if abs(dx) >= abs(dy):
        return A_PX if dx > 0 else (A_NX if dx < 0 else A_HOVER)
    else:
        return A_PY if dy > 0 else (A_NY if dy < 0 else A_HOVER)


def classical_eval(cfg, steps, seed):
    c = copy.deepcopy(cfg)
    c.seed = seed
    env = UAVIoTEnv(c)
    env.reset()

    # precompute oracle formation (connected, K-covering)
    pos, ck = search_best(env, iters=80, restarts=12,
                          rng=np.random.default_rng(seed + 7919),
                          connected_only=True)
    if pos is None:
        # fallback: spread UAVs evenly
        pos = np.array([[cfg.side * (i % 4 + 0.5) / 4.0,
                         cfg.side * (i // 4 + 0.5) / 2.0] for i in range(cfg.N)])
    formation = np.asarray(pos, dtype=float)

    # slot occupancy: which UAV is assigned to which slot
    slot_of = {}  # slot_idx -> uav_idx
    uav_slot = {}  # uav_idx -> slot_idx

    for _ in range(steps):
        acts = np.zeros(cfg.N, dtype=np.int64)
        active = list(env._active_idx())

        # determine who needs to charge
        charging = set()
        for i in active:
            if env.E[i] <= env.return_threshold(i):
                charging.add(i)

        # active UAVs that are not charging
        serving = [i for i in active if i not in charging]

        # assign serving UAVs to formation slots via Hungarian
        if serving and len(formation) >= len(serving):
            # build cost matrix: serving x slots
            # available slots = those not occupied by a charging UAV that's
            # still at its slot... simplify: all slots available
            cost = np.zeros((len(serving), len(formation)))
            for si, i in enumerate(serving):
                for sj in range(len(formation)):
                    cost[si, sj] = np.linalg.norm(env.p[i] - formation[sj])
            row_ind, col_ind = linear_sum_assignment(cost)
            for si, sj in zip(row_ind, col_ind):
                i = serving[si]
                slot = sj
                target = formation[slot]
                # move toward target
                if np.linalg.norm(env.p[i] - target) < 2.0:
                    acts[i] = A_HOVER
                else:
                    acts[i] = move_toward(env, i, target)

        # charging UAVs
        for i in charging:
            if np.min(np.linalg.norm(env.pads - env.p[i], axis=1)) <= cfg.pad_service_radius:
                acts[i] = A_CHARGE
            else:
                nearest_pad = env.pads[int(np.argmin(np.linalg.norm(env.pads - env.p[i], axis=1)))]
                acts[i] = move_toward(env, i, nearest_pad)

        # failed UAVs (mode -1) are handled by env
        obs, st, r, done, _ = env.step(acts)
        if done:
            break

    return env.summary()


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--tag", default="exp_ego_eval")
    ap.add_argument("--eval-offset", type=int, default=1000)
    ap.add_argument("--eval-seeds", type=int, default=5)
    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=500)
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="")
    a = ap.parse_args()

    # use base config (nominal)
    cfg = base_config()
    cfg.ego_sensors = True
    cfg.n_ego = 3
    cfg.dist_shape_kappa = 0.5
    cfg.use_w_penalty = True
    cfg.episode_len = a.steps

    scenes = list(range(a.eval_offset, a.eval_offset + a.eval_seeds))
    cks = []
    t0 = time.time()
    for s in scenes:
        summ = classical_eval(cfg, a.steps, s)
        cks.append(float(summ.get("CK", 0.0)))
        print("scene %d: CK=%.4f  [%.0fs]" % (s, cks[-1], time.time() - t0), flush=True)

    mean = float(np.mean(cks))
    sd = float(np.std(cks, ddof=1)) if len(cks) > 1 else 0.0
    print("\nclassical baseline: mean=%.4f (sd %.4f) over %d scenes" % (mean, sd, len(cks)))

    out = a.out or os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl", "runs", "classical_baseline.json")
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out), exist_ok=True)
    with open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"tag": "classical_baseline", "steps": a.steps, "scenes": scenes,
                   "per_scene": cks, "mean": mean, "sd": sd}, f, indent=1)
    print("wrote", out)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

==============================================================================
=== scripts/eval_agsc.py  (76 lines)
==============================================================================
"""Evaluate AG/SC (and CK) of a trained tag on held-out scenes.

The full held-out eval stores only CK statistics; the table also prints AG and
SC, so this script averages the three over the same held-out scene set.

Usage:
    python scripts/eval_agsc.py --tag exp_v2 --scenes 5 --steps 2000
"""
import argparse
import glob
import json
import os
import sys

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl"))
for v in ("OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS", "OMP_NUM_THREADS",
          "NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS", "VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS"):
    os.environ.setdefault(v, "1")

from uav_wireless.env import UAVIoTEnv
from uav_wireless.experiments import base_config, eval_agent
from uav_wireless.mappo import MAPPO


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--tag", default="exp_ego_fixed")
    ap.add_argument("--scenes", type=int, default=5)
    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=2000)
    ap.add_argument("--offset", type=int, default=1000)
    a = ap.parse_args()

    rundir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl",
                          "runs", a.tag)
    ckpts = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(rundir, "seed_*.pt")))
    scenes = list(range(a.offset, a.offset + a.scenes))
    out = []
    for ck in ckpts:
        meta = json.load(open(ck.replace(".pt", ".json"), encoding="utf-8"))
        c = base_config()
        c.ego_sensors = meta.get("ego", True)
        c.n_ego = meta.get("n_ego", 3)
        c.phy_ego_sensors = meta.get("phy_ego", False)
        c.use_critical_anchor = meta.get("anchor", False)
        c.use_scalable_id = meta.get("scalable_id", False)
        if "use_agent_id" in meta:
            c.use_agent_id = bool(meta["use_agent_id"])
        c.assist_return_mask = meta.get("assist_return", False)
        c.dist_shape_kappa = meta.get("shape", 0.0)
        c.use_w_penalty = meta.get("use_w", True)
        c.episode_len = a.steps
        env = UAVIoTEnv(c)
        ag = MAPPO(n_agents=c.N, obs_dim=env.obs_dim, state_dim=env.state_dim,
                   device="cpu")
        ag.load(ck)
        rows = [eval_agent(ag, c, steps=a.steps, seed=s, deterministic=False)
                for s in scenes]
        out.append({
            "ckpt": os.path.basename(ck),
            "CK": float(np.mean([r["CK"] for r in rows])),
            "AG": float(np.mean([r["AG"] for r in rows])),
            "SC": float(np.mean([r["SC"] for r in rows])),
        })
        print(ck, "CK", round(out[-1]["CK"], 4), "AG", round(out[-1]["AG"], 4),
              "SC", round(out[-1]["SC"], 4), flush=True)
    agg = {k: float(np.mean([o[k] for o in out])) for k in ("CK", "AG", "SC")}
    print("MEAN", {k: round(v, 4) for k, v in agg.items()})
    with open(os.path.join(rundir, "agsc.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump({"per_ckpt": out, "mean": agg, "scenes": scenes}, f, indent=1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

==============================================================================
=== scripts/compare_v1_v2.py  (75 lines)
==============================================================================
"""Compare the v2 retrain against the verified v1 headline.

Reads only stored artifacts; prints a paired per-seed comparison on the
held-out scenes, the AG/SC means, and the stress-regime table if a v2 stress
artifact exists.  No training and no evaluation.
"""
import json
import os

import numpy as np

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
ROOT = os.path.dirname(HERE)
RUNS = os.path.join(ROOT, "ml_impl", "runs")


def load(path):
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return json.load(f)


def headline(tag):
    p = os.path.join(RUNS, tag, "eval_heldout.json")
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        return None
    h = load(p)
    per = [float(v["mean"]) for v in h["per_checkpoint"].values()]
    return h, per


def main():
    v1 = headline("exp_ego_fixed")
    v2 = headline("exp_v2")
    if v1 is None:
        print("v1 eval_heldout missing")
        return 1
    h1, p1 = v1
    print("v1  CK=%.4f sd=%.4f n=%d" % (
        h1["mean_over_seeds"], h1["sd_over_seeds"], len(p1)))
    if v2 is None:
        print("v2 eval_heldout not ready")
        return 0
    h2, p2 = v2
    print("v2  CK=%.4f sd=%.4f n=%d" % (
        h2["mean_over_seeds"], h2["sd_over_seeds"], len(p2)))
    if len(p1) == len(p2):
        d = np.array(p2) - np.array(p1)
        from scipy import stats
        half = float(stats.t.ppf(0.975, len(d) - 1)
                     * np.std(d, ddof=1) / np.sqrt(len(d)))
        print("paired v2-v1: %+.4f  95%% CI [%+.4f, %+.4f]  per-seed %s"
              % (d.mean(), d.mean() - half, d.mean() + half,
                 ", ".join("%+.4f" % x for x in d)))
    for tag in ("exp_ego_fixed", "exp_v2"):
        p = os.path.join(RUNS, tag, "agsc.json")
        if os.path.exists(p):
            m = load(p)["mean"]
            print("%s AG=%.4f SC=%.4f" % (tag, m["AG"], m["SC"]))
    s2 = os.path.join(RUNS, "stress_eval", "stress_eval_v2.json")
    if os.path.exists(s2):
        reg = load(s2)["regimes"]
        print("\nstress regime          v2 model   best rule   delta    w/l")
        for name, r in reg.items():
            m = r.get("learner_mean")
            if m is None:
                continue
            b = r.get("best_rule_ck")
            print("%-22s %8.4f  %9.4f  %+7.4f   %s"
                  % (name, m, b, m - b, "MODEL" if m > b else "RULE"))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

==============================================================================
=== scripts/eval_scenarios_transfer.py  (128 lines)
==============================================================================
"""Zero-shot scenario transfer for a scalable-identity actor.

One policy trained on the nominal configuration is evaluated, without any
retraining, on every scenario variant.  This is only valid when the actor's
observation dimension is independent of N, M and the pad count, i.e. when the
checkpoint was trained with use_scalable_id=True.  The script checks that and
refuses to run otherwise.

Writes ml_impl/results_scenarios_transfer.csv and prints the win tally.
"""
import argparse
import copy
import csv
import glob
import json
import os
import sys

import numpy as np

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
ROOT = os.path.dirname(HERE)
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "ml_impl"))

from uav_wireless.env import UAVIoTEnv, obs_dim  # noqa: E402
from uav_wireless.experiments import (  # noqa: E402
    base_config, eval_agent, scenario_cfgs,
)
from uav_wireless.mappo import MAPPO  # noqa: E402
from uav_wireless.train import (  # noqa: E402
    baseline_reactive, baseline_reactive_local,
)


def actor_cfg(meta):
    c = base_config()
    c.ego_sensors = meta.get("ego", True)
    c.n_ego = meta.get("n_ego", 3)
    c.phy_ego_sensors = meta.get("phy_ego", False)
    c.use_critical_anchor = meta.get("anchor", False)
    c.use_scalable_id = meta.get("scalable_id", False)
    if "use_agent_id" in meta:
        c.use_agent_id = bool(meta["use_agent_id"])
    c.assist_return_mask = meta.get("assist_return", False)
    c.dist_shape_kappa = meta.get("shape", 0.0)
    return c


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--tag", default="exp_v2")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="ml_impl/results_scenarios_transfer.csv")
    ap.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=0,
                    help="evaluate only the first N variants (0 = all)")
    a = ap.parse_args()

    rundir = os.path.join(ROOT, "ml_impl", "runs", a.tag)
    ckpts = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(rundir, "seed_*.pt")))
    if not ckpts:
        print("no checkpoints in", rundir)
        return 1
    meta = json.load(open(ckpts[0].replace(".pt", ".json"), encoding="utf-8"))
    if not meta.get("scalable_id", False):
        print("checkpoints were not trained with use_scalable_id; "
              "zero-shot transfer across N/M/pads is not defined")
        return 1

    cfg_actor = actor_cfg(meta)
    env0 = UAVIoTEnv(cfg_actor)
    dim = env0.obs_dim
    agents = []
    for ck in ckpts:
        ag = MAPPO(n_agents=cfg_actor.N, obs_dim=dim,
                   state_dim=env0.state_dim, device="cpu")
        ag.load_actor(ck)
        agents.append(ag)

    rows = []
    wins = losses = 0
    variants = []
    for sid, question, cfgs in scenario_cfgs(base_config()):
        for vi, c0 in enumerate(cfgs):
            variants.append((sid, vi, question, c0))
    if a.limit:
        variants = variants[:a.limit]

    for sid, vi, question, c0 in variants:
        c = copy.deepcopy(c0)
        if obs_dim(c) != dim:
            print(f"skip {sid}.{vi}: obs_dim {obs_dim(c)} != {dim}")
            continue
        evs = [eval_agent(ag, c, steps=c.episode_len, seed=c.seed)
               for ag in agents]
        m = float(np.mean([e["CK"] for e in evs]))
        r = baseline_reactive(c, steps=c.episode_len, seed=c.seed)
        rl = baseline_reactive_local(c, steps=c.episode_len, seed=c.seed)
        won = m > rl["CK"]
        wins += int(won)
        losses += int(not won)
        variant = (f"N={c.N} M={c.M} Rcov={c.R_cov:.0f} "
                   f"Rcomm={c.R_comm:.0f} pads={c.n_pads} "
                   f"Bmax={c.B_max:.0f} fail={c.failure_enabled} "
                   f"clustered={c.start_clustered}")
        rows.append(dict(
            scenario=sid, variant=vi, question=question, config=variant,
            mappo_CK=m, mappo_sd=float(np.std([e["CK"] for e in evs], ddof=1)),
            react_CK=r["CK"], reactlocal_CK=rl["CK"],
            delta_vs_local=m - rl["CK"],
            winner="MODEL" if won else "RULE",
            protocol="zero-shot transfer, 5 seeds, 2000 steps",
            train_seeds=len(agents),
        ))
        print(f"{sid}.{vi} {variant}  model {m:.3f}  local {rl['CK']:.3f}  "
              f"{'MODEL' if won else 'RULE'}", flush=True)

    out = os.path.join(ROOT, a.out)
    with open(out, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(rows[0].keys()))
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(rows)
    print(f"\nvariant tally: MODEL {wins} - {losses} RULE")
    print("saved ->", out)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

==============================================================================
=== scripts/distill_actor.py  (131 lines)
==============================================================================
"""Distil the centralised planner into the decentralised actor (BC fine-tune).

Loads the (obs, actions) dataset produced by gen_planning_dataset.py and
fine-tunes each checkpoint's actor with supervised cross-entropy on the
planner's chosen actions.  The distilled actor is then decentralised: the
same local observation, one forward pass, no cloned simulator, no joint
search, no global state.  If even part of the planner's +0.021 survives,
the paper has a deployable claim instead of an oracle.

Run:
    python scripts/distill_actor.py --tag exp_ego_eval \
        --dataset ml_impl/runs/exp_ego_eval/planning_dataset.npz \
        --epochs 5 --lr 1e-4 --out-tag exp_distilled
"""
import argparse
import copy
import glob
import json
import os
import sys
import time

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl"))
for _v in ("OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS", "OMP_NUM_THREADS",
           "NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS", "VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS"):
    os.environ.setdefault(_v, "1")

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn

from uav_wireless.env import UAVIoTEnv
from uav_wireless.experiments import base_config
from uav_wireless.mappo import MAPPO


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--tag", default="exp_ego_eval")
    ap.add_argument("--dataset", default="")
    ap.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=5)
    ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=1e-4)
    ap.add_argument("--batch", type=int, default=2048)
    ap.add_argument("--out-tag", default="exp_distilled")
    ap.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, default=0, help="distil first K checkpoints (0 = all)")
    a = ap.parse_args()

    rundir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl", "runs", a.tag)
    ckpts = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(rundir, "seed_*.pt")))
    if a.seeds > 0:
        ckpts = ckpts[:a.seeds]
    if not ckpts:
        print("no checkpoints in", rundir)
        return 1
    dataset = a.dataset or os.path.join(rundir, "planning_dataset.npz")
    if not os.path.exists(dataset):
        print("dataset not found:", dataset)
        return 1

    d = np.load(dataset)
    obs_all = d["obs"].astype(np.float32)   # (T, N, obs_dim)
    acts_all = d["acts"].astype(np.int64)   # (T, N)
    # flatten to per-agent samples (o_i, a_i)
    obs_all = obs_all.reshape(-1, obs_all.shape[-1])
    acts_all = acts_all.reshape(-1)
    print("dataset: %d per-agent samples, obs_dim %d, n_actions %d"
          % (len(obs_all), obs_all.shape[1], acts_all.max() + 1))
    n = len(obs_all)

    outdir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl", "runs", a.out_tag)
    os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)

    for ck in ckpts:
        meta = json.load(open(ck.replace(".pt", ".json"), encoding="utf-8"))
        cfg = base_config()
        cfg.ego_sensors = meta.get("ego", False)
        cfg.n_ego = meta.get("n_ego", 3)
        cfg.dist_shape_kappa = meta.get("shape", 0.0)
        cfg.use_w_penalty = meta.get("use_w", True)
        cfg.use_critical_anchor = meta.get("anchor", False)
        cfg.episode_len = meta.get("n_steps", 2000)
        env = UAVIoTEnv(cfg)
        agent = MAPPO(n_agents=cfg.N, obs_dim=env.obs_dim,
                      state_dim=env.state_dim, device="cpu")
        agent.load(ck)

        actor = agent.actor
        opt = torch.optim.Adam(actor.parameters(), lr=a.lr)
        loss_fn = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()

        idx = np.arange(n)
        t0 = time.time()
        for epoch in range(a.epochs):
            np.random.shuffle(idx)
            total = 0.0
            nbatch = 0
            for start in range(0, n, a.batch):
                sel = idx[start:start + a.batch]
                obs_b = torch.as_tensor(obs_all[sel], dtype=torch.float32)
                act_b = torch.as_tensor(acts_all[sel], dtype=torch.int64)
                logits = actor(obs_b)  # (B, n_actions)
                loss = loss_fn(logits, act_b)
                opt.zero_grad()
                loss.backward()
                opt.step()
                total += float(loss.item())
                nbatch += 1
            print("  %s epoch %d: loss %.4f  [%.0fs]"
                  % (os.path.basename(ck), epoch + 1, total / nbatch,
                     time.time() - t0), flush=True)

        # save distilled checkpoint (same dict shape as MAPPO.save)
        name = os.path.basename(ck)
        out_pt = os.path.join(outdir, name)
        torch.save(dict(actor=actor.state_dict(), critic=agent.critic.state_dict(),
                        value_norm=agent.value_norm,
                        ret_mean=agent._ret_mean, ret_std=agent._ret_std),
                   out_pt)
        # copy the metadata json so eval_ckpt.py can restore the config
        import shutil
        shutil.copyfile(ck.replace(".pt", ".json"),
                        os.path.join(outdir, name.replace(".pt", ".json")))
        print("  distilled ->", out_pt, flush=True)

    print("\nDONE: distilled checkpoints in", outdir)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

==============================================================================
=== scripts/energy_certificate.py  (245 lines)
==============================================================================
"""
Energy-flow feasibility certificate for persistent K-coverage.

Extends the spatial packing certificate (feasibility.py) with an energy and
charging-throughput bound.  The spatial certificate asks "how many UAVs are
needed to K-cover the region"; this one asks "given the battery and pad
budget, how many UAV-seconds can the fleet actually spend airborne, and
therefore what is the maximum time-averaged K-coverage it can sustain".

Three quantities are reported:

  1. Steady-state bound (T -> infinity): the fleet can keep airborne at most
     S * P_pad_net / P_air UAVs, so persistent K-coverage needs
     N_min <= S * P_pad_net / P_air.  If this fails, no policy, however
     clever, can sustain the mission; the configuration is not persistent.

  2. Finite-horizon bound (T = episode length): even over a finite mission
     the airborne UAV-seconds are capped by (stored energy + pad energy) / P_air.

  3. Energy-aware ceiling: a small linear-program (fluid relaxation) that
     maximises the time-averaged airborne fleet (and hence an upper bound on
     CK) subject to the per-UAV energy budget, the pad throughput, and the
     no-fly-while-charging constraint.  It is the energy analogue of the
     teleporting placement oracle.

Usage:
    python scripts/energy_certificate.py
    python scripts/energy_certificate.py --T 2000 --n-pads 4 --B-max 60000
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import sys

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import linprog

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl"))
from uav_wireless.env import SimConfig


def params(cfg: SimConfig) -> dict:
    # The mission is a coverage mission: the fleet flies, it does not hover in
    # place.  The airborne power is therefore the FLIGHT draw; the hover draw
    # is reported alongside as the optimistic bound.  Using hover as P_air
    # (the previous revision) understated the power by ~30% and made the
    # steady-state ceiling ~45% too high.
    p_air_flight = cfg.P_move + cfg.P_elec + cfg.P_tx
    p_air_hover = cfg.P_hover + cfg.P_elec + cfg.P_tx
    p_air = p_air_flight
    p_pad_gross = cfg.charge_rate * cfg.B_max / cfg.dt
    p_pad_net = p_pad_gross - (cfg.P_elec + cfg.P_tx)
    n_min = 4 * cfg.K
    return dict(
        N=cfg.N, K=cfg.K, M=cfg.M, side=cfg.side,
        B_max=cfg.B_max, E_reserve=cfg.E_reserve,
        charge_rate=cfg.charge_rate, n_pads=cfg.n_pads,
        p_air=p_air, p_air_flight=p_air_flight, p_air_hover=p_air_hover,
        p_pad_gross=p_pad_gross, p_pad_net=p_pad_net,
        n_min=n_min, dt=cfg.dt, episode_len=cfg.episode_len,
    )


def steady_state(p: dict) -> dict:
    n_air_ss = p["n_pads"] * p["p_pad_net"] / p["p_air"]
    ceiling_ss = min(1.0, n_air_ss / p["n_min"])
    feasible = n_air_ss >= p["n_min"]
    return dict(n_air_ss=n_air_ss, ceiling_ss=ceiling_ss, feasible=feasible,
                deficit=p["n_min"] - n_air_ss)


def finite_horizon(p: dict, T: float, e0_frac: float = 1.0) -> dict:
    e_stored = p["N"] * e0_frac * p["B_max"]
    e_pad = p["n_pads"] * p["p_pad_net"] * T
    e_usable = e_stored + e_pad - p["N"] * p["E_reserve"]
    air_seconds = max(0.0, e_usable / p["p_air"])
    ceiling = min(1.0, air_seconds / (p["n_min"] * T))
    return dict(T=T, e_stored=e_stored, e_pad=e_pad, e_usable=e_usable,
                air_seconds=air_seconds, ceiling=ceiling,
                avg_airborne=air_seconds / T)


def energy_lp(p: dict, T: float, e0_frac: float = 1.0) -> dict:
    N = p["N"]
    E0 = e0_frac * p["B_max"]
    reserve = p["E_reserve"]
    P_air = p["p_air"]
    P_pad = p["p_pad_net"]
    S = p["n_pads"]

    nvar = 2 * N
    c_obj = np.zeros(nvar)
    c_obj[:N] = -1.0

    A_ub = []
    b_ub = []
    for i in range(N):
        row = np.zeros(nvar)
        row[i] = 1.0
        row[N + i] = 1.0
        A_ub.append(row)
        b_ub.append(1.0)
    row = np.zeros(nvar)
    row[N:] = 1.0
    A_ub.append(row)
    b_ub.append(float(S))
    for i in range(N):
        row = np.zeros(nvar)
        row[i] = P_air * T
        row[N + i] = -P_pad * T
        A_ub.append(row)
        b_ub.append(E0 - reserve)

    A_ub = np.array(A_ub)
    b_ub = np.array(b_ub)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * nvar

    res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    if not res.success:
        return dict(success=False, message=res.message)
    a = res.x[:N]
    c = res.x[N:]
    air = float(a.sum())
    ceiling = min(1.0, air / p["n_min"])
    return dict(success=True, air=air, ceiling=ceiling, a=[round(x, 3) for x in a],
                c=[round(x, 3) for x in c], n_min=p["n_min"], status=res.status)


def report(cfg: SimConfig, T: float, e0_frac: float = 1.0) -> dict:
    p = params(cfg)
    ss = steady_state(p)
    fh = finite_horizon(p, T, e0_frac)
    lp = energy_lp(p, T, e0_frac)
    return dict(params=p, steady_state=ss, finite_horizon=fh, lp=lp)


def print_report(r: dict, label: str = "") -> None:
    p = r["params"]
    ss = r["steady_state"]
    fh = r["finite_horizon"]
    lp = r["lp"]
    print("=" * 78)
    if label:
        print(label)
    print(f"N={p['N']} K={p['K']} M={p['M']} side={p['side']} n_pads={p['n_pads']} "
          f"B_max={p['B_max']:.0f} charge_rate={p['charge_rate']} E_reserve={p['E_reserve']:.0f}")
    print(f"P_air (flight+elec+tx) = {p['p_air_flight']:.2f} W   "
          f"hover-only bound = {p['p_air_hover']:.2f} W")
    print(f"P_pad gross = {p['p_pad_gross']:.2f} W   net = {p['p_pad_net']:.2f} W")
    print(f"Spatial certificate: N_min = 4K = {p['n_min']} UAVs needed for full K-coverage")
    print()
    print("Steady-state (T -> inf):")
    print(f"  max sustainable airborne UAVs = S*P_pad_net/P_air = "
          f"{p['n_pads']}*{p['p_pad_net']:.2f}/{p['p_air']:.2f} = {ss['n_air_ss']:.3f}")
    print(f"  persistent CK ceiling = {ss['ceiling_ss']:.3f}   "
          f"feasible = {ss['feasible']}   deficit = {ss['deficit']:.2f} UAVs")
    print()
    print(f"Finite horizon T = {fh['T']:.0f} s (e0_frac = {fh['e_stored']/p['N']/p['B_max']:.2f} of full charge):")
    print(f"  stored = {fh['e_stored']/1000:.1f} kJ   pad = {fh['e_pad']/1000:.1f} kJ   "
          f"usable = {fh['e_usable']/1000:.1f} kJ")
    print(f"  airborne UAV-seconds = {fh['air_seconds']:.0f}   avg airborne = {fh['avg_airborne']:.2f}   "
          f"ceiling = {fh['ceiling']:.3f}")
    print()
    print("Energy-aware LP ceiling (fluid relaxation):")
    if lp.get("success"):
        print(f"  max avg airborne UAVs = {lp['air']:.3f}   ceiling CK = {lp['ceiling']:.3f}")
        print(f"  a (airborne frac per UAV) = {lp['a']}")
        print(f"  c (charging frac per UAV) = {lp['c']}")
    else:
        print("  LP failed:", lp.get("message"))
    print()


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--T", type=float, default=None, help="mission length in seconds (default: episode_len*dt)")
    ap.add_argument("--n-pads", type=int, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--B-max", type=float, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--charge-rate", type=float, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--N", type=int, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--e0-frac", type=float, default=1.0, help="initial charge fraction (1.0 = full)")
    ap.add_argument("--sweep", action="store_true", help="sweep a few alternative configs")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="")
    a = ap.parse_args()

    def make_cfg(overrides: dict) -> SimConfig:
        base = SimConfig(N=8, M=20, K=2, side=80.0, R_cov=35.0, R_comm=40.0,
                         episode_len=2000, ego_sensors=True, n_ego=3)
        for k, v in overrides.items():
            setattr(base, k, v)
        return base

    overrides = {}
    if a.n_pads is not None: overrides["n_pads"] = a.n_pads
    if a.B_max is not None: overrides["B_max"] = a.B_max
    if a.charge_rate is not None: overrides["charge_rate"] = a.charge_rate
    if a.N is not None: overrides["N"] = a.N

    cfg = make_cfg(overrides)
    T = a.T if a.T is not None else cfg.episode_len * cfg.dt

    r = report(cfg, T, a.e0_frac)
    print_report(r, label="CONFIG UNDER TEST")

    if a.sweep:
        print("\n" + "#" * 78)
        print("SWEEP: alternative configurations")
        print("#" * 78)
        variants = [
            ("baseline (N=8, S=2, B=30k, cr=0.0002)", {}),
            ("more pads (S=4)", {"n_pads": 4}),
            ("bigger packs (B=60k)", {"B_max": 60000.0}),
            ("faster charge (cr=0.0004)", {"charge_rate": 0.0004}),
            ("more pads + faster charge", {"n_pads": 4, "charge_rate": 0.0004}),
            ("more UAVs (N=12)", {"N": 12}),
            ("more pads + bigger packs", {"n_pads": 4, "B_max": 60000.0}),
            ("all three (N=12, S=4, B=60k, cr=0.0004)", {"N": 12, "n_pads": 4, "B_max": 60000.0, "charge_rate": 0.0004}),
        ]
        rows = []
        for label, ov in variants:
            c = make_cfg(ov)
            rr = report(c, T, a.e0_frac)
            ss = rr["steady_state"]
            lp = rr["lp"]
            rows.append((label, c.N, c.n_pads, c.B_max, c.charge_rate,
                         ss["n_air_ss"], ss["ceiling_ss"], ss["feasible"],
                         lp.get("ceiling", float("nan"))))
        print(f"{'variant':42s} {'N':>3s} {'S':>2s} {'Bmax':>6s} {'cr':>7s} {'ss_air':>7s} {'ss_ceil':>7s} {'feas':>5s} {'lp_ceil':>7s}")
        for row in rows:
            print(f"{row[0]:42s} {row[1]:3d} {row[2]:2d} {row[3]:6.0f} {row[4]:7.4f} "
                  f"{row[5]:7.3f} {row[6]:7.3f} {str(row[7]):>5s} {row[8]:7.3f}")

    out = a.out or os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl", "runs", "energy_certificate.json")
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out), exist_ok=True)
    with open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"T": T, "e0_frac": a.e0_frac, "report": r}, f, indent=1, default=str)
    print("wrote", out)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

==============================================================================
=== scripts/export_policy_trace.py  (75 lines)
==============================================================================
"""Export a learned-policy trajectory as a BonnMotion trace for INET/Qtenv.

The demo replays the frozen actor from a checkpoint, step by step, and writes
one line per UAV as (t x y) triples at the simulator's control period.  This is
the trace the OMNeT++ visualization should show: the swarm starting in its
clustered deployment and spreading across the field under the learned policy.
"""
import os
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl"))
for v in ("OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS", "OMP_NUM_THREADS",
          "NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS", "VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS"):
    os.environ.setdefault(v, "1")

import numpy as np

from uav_wireless.env import UAVIoTEnv
from uav_wireless.experiments import base_config
from uav_wireless.mappo import MAPPO

CKPT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl", "runs",
                    "exp_ego_eval", "seed_0.pt")
OUT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "Simulation", "omnet_uav",
                   "traces", "episode_policy.movements")
STEPS = 200      # 200 x 0.2 s = 40 s, matching the INET sim-time-limit
SEED = 7         # a scene where the policy deploys rather than hovers


def main():
    cfg = base_config()
    cfg.ego_sensors = True
    cfg.n_ego = 3
    cfg.dist_shape_kappa = 0.5
    cfg.episode_len = STEPS
    cfg.seed = SEED
    env = UAVIoTEnv(cfg)
    agent = MAPPO(n_agents=cfg.N, obs_dim=env.obs_dim,
                  state_dim=env.state_dim, device="cpu")
    agent.load(CKPT)

    obs, _ = env.reset()
    traj = [[(0.0, float(env.p[i, 0]), float(env.p[i, 1]))]
            for i in range(cfg.N)]
    for t in range(1, STEPS + 1):
        mask = np.stack(env.action_masks())
        acts = agent.act(obs, mask, deterministic=True)
        obs, _state, _r, done, _ = env.step(acts)
        for i in range(cfg.N):
            traj[i].append((t * cfg.dt, float(env.p[i, 0]), float(env.p[i, 1])))
        if done:
            break

    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(OUT), exist_ok=True)
    with open(OUT, "w") as f:
        for i in range(cfg.N):
            f.write(" ".join(f"{t:.3f} {x:.3f} {y:.3f}"
                             for t, x, y in traj[i]) + "\n")

    s = env.summary()
    xs = np.array([p[1] for p in traj[0]])
    ys = np.array([p[2] for p in traj[0]])
    print(f"wrote {OUT}")
    print(f"steps={len(traj[0])-1} CK={s.get('CK', 0):.3f} AG={s.get('AG', 0):.3f}")
    print(f"UAV0 path: start ({traj[0][0][1]:.1f},{traj[0][0][2]:.1f}) "
          f"end ({traj[0][-1][1]:.1f},{traj[0][-1][2]:.1f})")
    allx = np.concatenate([[p[1] for p in tr] for tr in traj])
    ally = np.concatenate([[p[2] for p in tr] for tr in traj])
    print(f"field span: x {allx.min():.1f}-{allx.max():.1f}, "
          f"y {ally.min():.1f}-{ally.max():.1f}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

==============================================================================
=== scripts/gen_moving_trace.py  (70 lines)
==============================================================================
import os, sys, json
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl"))
for v in ("OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS","MKL_NUM_THREADS","OMP_NUM_THREADS","NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS","VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS"):
    os.environ.setdefault(v, "1")
import numpy as np
from uav_wireless.env import UAVIoTEnv
from uav_wireless.experiments import base_config
from uav_wireless.mappo import MAPPO

cfg = base_config()
cfg.episode_len = 200
cfg.failure_enabled = False

# load first MAPPO checkpoint deterministically
ck = sorted(os.listdir(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl", "runs", "exp_ego_eval")))
ckpts = [os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "ml_impl", "runs", "exp_ego_eval", f) for f in ck if f.endswith(".pt")]
print("ckpts", ckpts)
import torch
agent = MAPPO(obs_dim=68, n_actions=6 if False else None, device="cpu") if False else None

# simpler: use greedy move-toward-nearest-deficit heuristic
env = UAVIoTEnv(cfg)
env.cfg.seed = 1000
obs, _ = env.reset()
trajs = [[] for _ in range(cfg.N)]
t = 0.0
for ui in range(cfg.N):
    trajs[ui].append((t, float(env.p[ui,0]), float(env.p[ui,1])))
ncov = env.coverage_counts()
for step in range(200):
    acts = [0]*cfg.N
    ncov = env.coverage_counts()
    deficient = [d for d in range(cfg.M) if ncov[d] < cfg.K]
    for i in range(cfg.N):
        if not deficient:
            acts[i] = 0
            continue
        dists = [(float(np.linalg.norm(env.sensors[d]-env.p[i])), d) for d in deficient]
        dists.sort()
        target = env.sensors[dists[0][1]]
        dx = target[0]-env.p[i,0]; dy = target[1]-env.p[i,1]
        if abs(dx) >= abs(dy):
            acts[i] = 1 if dx>0 else 2
        else:
            acts[i] = 3 if dy>0 else 4
        if np.linalg.norm([dx,dy]) < 2.0:
            acts[i] = 0
    res = env.step(np.array(acts))
    obs = res[0]
    t += cfg.dt
    for ui in range(cfg.N):
        trajs[ui].append((t, float(env.p[ui,0]), float(env.p[ui,1])))
    if res[3]:
        break

# write
out_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "Simulation", "omnet_uav", "traces")
os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
out_file = os.path.join(out_dir, "episode_moving.movements")
with open(out_file, "w") as f:
    for ui in range(cfg.N):
        parts = []
        for (tt, x, y) in trajs[ui]:
            parts.append(f"{tt:.3f} {x:.3f} {y:.3f}")
        f.write(" ".join(parts) + "\n")
print("wrote", out_file)
print("start spread (first UAVs):", trajs[0][:3])
print("end (last):", trajs[0][-3:])
print("summary:", env.summary().get("CK"))

=== scripts/verify_report_numbers.py  (208 lines)
